Title: Galwad at Orseddfaingc y Gras; fel na'n condemnir o flaen Gorseddfaingc Barn: neu, Ymddiddanion ynghylch Gweddi; annogaethau a chyfarwyddiadau i weddio Duw; yr ail argraphiad, gan Weinidog o Eglwys Loegr
Creator: Griffith Jones of Llanddowror (the host's attribution; the title page names the author only as 'a minister of the Church of England')
Publisher: Argraphwyd gan John Oliver, yn y lle a elwir Bartholomew Close, ger llaw West Smithfield (London)
Publication Date: 1750 (the host's date; the year is not legible in the scan of this second edition)
Rights: Public Domain (published 1750, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: cym
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_galwad-at-orseddfaingc-y_jones-griffith_1750 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_galwad-at-orseddfaingc-y_jones-griffith_1750), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_galwad-at-orseddfaingc-y_jones-griffith_1750_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1750. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 43ab8de18b1ad7a4a38732b649b8383fbada6edf7c4a99d8cdd7c381d5ef29e0.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan; the title page is noisy, the dialogues readable with the long s kept. The volume opens with a Welsh alphabet for learners, as the circulating schools' books did.
Content note: A Welsh catechetical book on prayer in dialogue form, issued for the readers of the circulating schools; the plain Welsh devotional prose the revival's converts were taught to read.
